c2:["$","p",null,{"className":"mt-3 text-sm text-muted leading-6","children":"The count is the largest number the display can show before it switches range. A 6,000-count meter reads up to 5,999, so a 5 V rail shows as 5.000 V on its 6 V range. A 4,000-count meter tops out at 3,999, so the same 5 V lands on the 40 V range and reads 5.00 V, one digit less. For most household work either is fine; for electronics, the extra digit helps."}]
